title: "Documenso API reference"
sidebarTitle: "API"
description: "API reference for the Documenso plugin in Corsair: every `documenso.api.*` operation with its input and output types, taken from the plugin's Zod schemas."
---

<Info>
**New to Corsair?** See [API access](/concepts/api), [authentication](/concepts/auth), and [error handling](/concepts/error-handling).
</Info>

## Documents

### attachments.create.post

`documents.attachments.create.post`

POST /document/attachment/create — [Deprecated] Create attachment.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.documents.attachments.create.post({
	documentId: 1,
	data: { label: 'sample', data: 'https://documenso.invalid/resource' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `documentId` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object` | Yes | — |

Deprecated Documenso v2 route (`POST /document/attachment/create`); use the corresponding envelope-based operation for new integrations.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  label: string,
  data: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



---

### attachments.delete.post

`documents.attachments.delete.post`

POST /document/attachment/delete — [Deprecated] Delete attachment.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.documents.attachments.delete.post({ id: 'sample' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |

Deprecated Documenso v2 route (`POST /document/attachment/delete`); use the corresponding envelope-based operation for new integrations.



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |



---

### attachments.find.get

`documents.attachments.find.get`

GET /document/attachment — [Deprecated] Find attachments. Documenso's server currently routes this path to the get-document handler and answers 400; use envelopes.attachments.find with the document's envelopeId instead.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.documents.attachments.find.get({ documentId: 1 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `documentId` | `number` | Yes | — |

Deprecated Documenso v2 route (`GET /document/attachment`); use the corresponding envelope-based operation for new integrations.



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id: string,
  type: link,
  label: string,
  data: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### attachments.update.post

`documents.attachments.update.post`

POST /document/attachment/update — [Deprecated] Update attachment.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.documents.attachments.update.post({
	id: 'sample',
	data: { label: 'sample', data: 'https://documenso.invalid/resource' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object` | Yes | — |

Deprecated Documenso v2 route (`POST /document/attachment/update`); use the corresponding envelope-based operation for new integrations.

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  label: string,
  data: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |

### create.post

`documents.create.post`

POST /document/create — [Deprecated] Create document.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.documents.create.post({
	payload: { title: 'sample' },
	file: { filename: 'contract.pdf', contentType: 'application/pdf', contentBase64: 'JVBERi0xLjQK' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `payload` | `object` | Yes | — |
| `file` | `object` | Yes | — |

Deprecated Documenso v2 route (`POST /document/create`); use the corresponding envelope-based operation for new integrations.

### createV0.post

`documents.createV0.post`

POST /document/create/beta — [Deprecated] Create document.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.documents.createV0.post({ title: 'sample' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `title` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `externalId` | `string` | No | — |
| `visibility` | `EVERYONE \| MANAGER_AND_ABOVE \| ADMIN` | No | — |
| `globalAccessAuth` | `(ACCOUNT \| TWO_FACTOR_AUTH)[]` | No | — |
| `globalActionAuth` | `(ACCOUNT \| PASSKEY \| TWO_FACTOR_AUTH \| PASSWORD)[]` | No | — |
| `formValues` | `object` | No | — |
| `folderId` | `string` | No | — |
| `recipients` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `attachments` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `meta` | `object` | No | — |

Deprecated Documenso v2 route (`POST /document/create/beta`); use the corresponding envelope-based operation for new integrations.

## Envelopes

### attachments.create.post

`envelopes.attachments.create.post`

POST /envelope/attachment/create — Create attachment.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.envelopes.attachments.create.post({
	envelopeId: 'sample',
	data: { label: 'sample', data: 'https://documenso.invalid/resource' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `envelopeId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  label: string,
  data: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



---

### attachments.delete.post

`envelopes.attachments.delete.post`

POST /envelope/attachment/delete — Delete attachment.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.envelopes.attachments.delete.post({ id: 'sample' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |



---

### attachments.find.get

`envelopes.attachments.find.get`

GET /envelope/attachment — Find attachments.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.envelopes.attachments.find.get({ envelopeId: 'sample' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `envelopeId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `token` | `string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[]` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  id: string,
  type: link,
  label: string,
  data: string
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### attachments.update.post

`envelopes.attachments.update.post`

POST /envelope/attachment/update — Update attachment.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.envelopes.attachments.update.post({
	id: 'sample',
	data: { label: 'sample', data: 'https://documenso.invalid/resource' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `data` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="data full type">

```ts
{
  label: string,
  data: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `boolean` | Yes | — |

### auditLogDownloadPdf.get

`envelopes.auditLogDownloadPdf.get`

GET /envelope/&#123;envelopeId&#125;/audit-log/download — Download envelope audit log PDF.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.envelopes.auditLogDownloadPdf.get({ envelopeId: 'sample' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `envelopeId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `kind` | `binary` | Yes | — |
| `contentType` | `application/pdf` | Yes | — |
| `base64` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `size` | `number` | Yes | — |



---

### auditLogFind.get

`envelopes.auditLogFind.get`

GET /envelope/&#123;envelopeId&#125;/audit-log — Get envelope audit logs.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.documenso.api.envelopes.auditLogFind.get({ envelopeId: 'sample' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `envelopeId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `page` | `number` | No | — |
| `perPage` | `number` | No | — |
| `orderByColumn` | `createdAt` | No | — |
| `orderByDirection` | `asc \| desc` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `data` | `object[]` | Yes | — |
| `count` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `currentPage` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `perPage` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `totalPages` | `number` | Yes | — |

### items.createMany.post

	payload: { envelopeId: 'sample' },

});
